00169: def textured_material(name,base,scale,rough=.85,grain=False):
00170:     m = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00171:     m.use_nodes = True
00172:     n,l = m.node_tree.nodes,m.node_tree.links
00173:     bs = n.get('Principled BSDF')
00174:     bs.inputs['Roughness'].default_value = rough
00175:     tex = n.new('ShaderNodeTexNoise')
00176:     tex.inputs['Scale'].default_value = scale
00177:     tex.inputs['Detail'].default_value = 3
00178:     coord = n.new('ShaderNodeTexCoord')
00179:     if grain:
00180:         mapping = n.new('ShaderNodeVectorMath')
00181:         mapping.operation = 'MULTIPLY'
00182:         mapping.inputs[1].default_value = (5,.24,3)
00183:         l.new(coord.outputs['Generated'],mapping.inputs[0])
00184:         l.new(mapping.outputs[0],tex.inputs['Vector'])
00185:     else:
00186:         l.new(coord.outputs['Generated'],tex.inputs['Vector'])

00188:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position = .18
00189:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = tuple(v*.64 for v in base)+(1,)
00190:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position = .82
00191:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = tuple(min(1,v*1.28) for v in base)+(1,)
00192:     l.new(tex.outputs['Fac'],ramp.inputs[0])
00193:     l.new(ramp.outputs[0],bs.inputs['Base Color'])
00194:     bump = n.new('ShaderNodeBump')
00195:     bump.inputs['Strength'].default_value = .19
00196:     bump.inputs['Distance'].default_value = .012 if grain else .035
00197:     l.new(tex.outputs['Fac'],bump.inputs['Height'])
00198:     l.new(bump.outputs[0],bs.inputs['Normal'])
00199:     return m

00171: def textured_material(name,base,scale,rough=.85,grain=False):
00172:     m=bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00173:     m.use_nodes=True
00174:     n=m.node_tree.nodes
00175:     l=m.node_tree.links
00176:     bs=n.get('Principled BSDF')
00177:     bs.inputs['Roughness'].default_value=rough
00178:     tex=n.new('ShaderNodeTexNoise')
00179:     tex.inputs['Scale'].default_value=scale
00180:     tex.inputs['Detail'].default_value=3
00181:     coord=n.new('ShaderNodeTexCoord')
00182:     if grain:
00183:         mapping=n.new('ShaderNodeVectorMath')
00184:         mapping.operation='MULTIPLY'
00185:         mapping.inputs[1].default_value=(5,.24,3)
00186:         l.new(coord.outputs['Generated'],mapping.inputs[0])
00187:         l.new(mapping.outputs[0],tex.inputs['Vector'])
00188:     else:
00189:         l.new(coord.outputs['Generated'],tex.inputs['Vector'])
00190:     ramp=n.new('ShaderNodeValToRGB')
00191:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position=.18
00192:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color=tuple(v*.64 for v in base)+(1,)
00193:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position=.82
00194:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color=tuple(min(1,v*1.28) for v in base)+(1,)
00195:     l.new(tex.outputs['Fac'],ramp.inputs[0])
00196:     l.new(ramp.outputs[0],bs.inputs['Base Color'])
00197:     bump=n.new('ShaderNodeBump')
00198:     bump.inputs['Strength'].default_value=.19
00199:     bump.inputs['Distance'].default_value=.012 if grain else .035
00200:     l.new(tex.outputs['Fac'],bump.inputs['Height'])
00201:     l.new(bump.outputs[0],bs.inputs['Normal'])
00202:     return m
